##### Interactions with fellow spouses/concubines/consorts of the same partner
# by Silverswee(e)per

namespace = tianxia

# IDs 2201-2700 reserved



##### Interaction events

# TIANXIATODO: Portraits, including show_portrait
# TIANXIATODO: Opinion modifiers where relevant
# TIANXIATODO: Trait changes
# TIANXIATODO: More options for the shared spouse if they disapprove of witchcraft punishments by target/"target"
# TIANXIATODO: Kinslaying where relevant
# TIANXIATODO: Break up with spouse/consort acted against unless it's in a minor fashion
# TIANXIATODO: Grace penalties
# TIANXIATODO: Murder (attempt) fallout
# TIANXIATODO: Event pictures, borders
# TIANXIATODO: Make sure event chains still make sense if either party gets imprisoned/etc.
# TIANXIATODO: Event delays
# TIANXIATODO: Sanity checks for incest and a co-consort being the child or parent of the other consort
# TIANXIATODO: Poison knowledge, where relevant
# TIANXIATODO: PR and GC blockers on replacement
# TIANXIATODO: Checks on possible lovers being good candidates
# TIANXIATODO: Standardize propositioning piety/trait effects
# TIANXIATODO: Lover fallout
# TIANXIATODO: Inform shared partner of cursing even if they can't do anything
# TIANXIATODO: Let the shared partner disapprove of the curse target punishing the other spouse/consort
# TIANXIATODO: Better masking of whether an accusation is fake or not by changing the accusation events to have the same setup as honest suspicion of not-real witchcraft
# TIANXIATODO: Spouses/consorts with higher tier relatives more prone to seeking to elevate themselves/putting others in their place, particularly if the other party is Lowborn or close to

### Witchcraft - real attempt to curse someone

# DW spouse/consort might use witchcraft to try to curse a fellow spouse/consort in a few different ways
character_event = {
	id = tianxia.2201
	desc = EVTDESC_TIANXIA_2201
	picture = GFX_evt_garden # TIANXIATODO
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion # TIANXIATODO
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	min_age = 16
	has_dlc = "Mystics"
	is_in_society = yes
	prisoner = no
	
	trigger = {
		is_devil_worshiper_trigger = yes
		has_society_currency_medium_trigger = yes # Cheaper than Tainted Touch as it'll not hurt the target's health
		is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
		OR = {
			AND = {
				is_married = yes
				any_spouse = {
					NOR = {
						is_rival = ROOT
						reverse_has_opinion_modifier = {
							who = ROOT
							name = opinion_abducted
						}
					}
					OR = {
						any_spouse = {
							is_adult = yes
							NOT = {
								character = ROOT
							}
							is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
							NOR = {
								is_friend = ROOT
								is_lover = ROOT
							}
						}
						any_consort = {
							is_adult = yes
							is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
							NOR = {
								is_friend = ROOT
								is_lover = ROOT
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
			AND = {
				is_consort = yes
				is_senior_consort_party = no
				consort = {
					NOR = {
						is_rival = ROOT
						reverse_has_opinion_modifier = {
							who = ROOT
							name = opinion_abducted
						}
					}
					OR = {
						any_spouse = {
							is_adult = yes
							is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
							NOR = {
								is_friend = ROOT
								is_lover = ROOT
							}
						}
						any_consort = {
							is_adult = yes
							NOT = {
								character = ROOT
							}
							is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
							NOR = {
								is_friend = ROOT
								is_lover = ROOT
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	
	immediate = {
		clr_character_flag = dw_curse_ugliness
		clr_character_flag = dw_curse_infertility
		clr_character_flag = dw_curse_misfortune
		
		if = {
			limit = {
				is_married = yes
			}
			
			random_spouse = {
				limit = {
					NOR = {
						is_rival = ROOT
						reverse_has_opinion_modifier = {
							who = ROOT
							name = opinion_abducted
						}
					}
					OR = {
						any_spouse = {
							is_adult = yes
							NOT = {
								character = ROOT
							}
							is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
							NOR = {
								is_friend = ROOT
								is_lover = ROOT
							}
						}
						any_consort = {
							is_adult = yes
							NOT = {
								character = ROOT
							}
							is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
							NOR = {
								is_friend = ROOT
								is_lover = ROOT
							}
						}
					}
				}
				
				preferred_limit = {
					OR = {
						any_spouse = {
							is_adult = yes
							NOT = {
								character = ROOT
							}
							is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
							is_rival = ROOT
						}
						any_consort = {
							is_adult = yes
							NOT = {
								character = ROOT
							}
							is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
							is_rival = ROOT
						}
					}
				}
				
				save_event_target_as = shared_partner
				
				random_list = {
					50 = {
						trigger = {
							any_spouse = {
								is_adult = yes
								NOT = {
									character = ROOT
								}
								is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
								NOR = {
									is_friend = ROOT
									is_lover = ROOT
								}
							}
						}
						
						random_spouse = {
							limit = {
								is_adult = yes
								NOT = {
									character = ROOT
								}
								is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
								NOR = {
									is_friend = ROOT
									is_lover = ROOT
								}
							}
							
							preferred_limit = {
								is_rival = ROOT
							}
							
							save_event_target_as = curse_target
						}
					}
					
					50 = {
						trigger = {
							any_consort = {
								is_adult = yes
								NOT = {
									character = ROOT
								}
								is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
								NOR = {
									is_friend = ROOT
									is_lover = ROOT
								}
							}
						}
						
						random_consort = {
							limit = {
								is_adult = yes
								NOT = {
									character = ROOT
								}
								is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
								NOR = {
									is_friend = ROOT
									is_lover = ROOT
								}
							}
							
							preferred_limit = {
								is_rival = ROOT
							}
							
							save_event_target_as = curse_target
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		
		else = {
			consort = {
				save_event_target_as = shared_partner
				
				random_list = {
					50 = {
						trigger = {
							any_spouse = {
								is_adult = yes
								NOT = {
									character = ROOT
								}
								is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
								NOR = {
									is_friend = ROOT
									is_lover = ROOT
								}
							}
						}
						
						random_spouse = {
							limit = {
								is_adult = yes
								NOT = {
									character = ROOT
								}
								is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
								NOR = {
									is_friend = ROOT
									is_lover = ROOT
								}
							}
							
							preferred_limit = {
								is_rival = ROOT
							}
							
							save_event_target_as = curse_target
						}
					}
					
					50 = {
						trigger = {
							any_consort = {
								is_adult = yes
								NOT = {
									character = ROOT
								}
								is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
								NOR = {
									is_friend = ROOT
									is_lover = ROOT
								}
							}
						}
						
						random_consort = {
							limit = {
								is_adult = yes
								NOT = {
									character = ROOT
								}
								is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
								NOR = {
									is_friend = ROOT
									is_lover = ROOT
								}
							}
							
							preferred_limit = {
								is_rival = ROOT
							}
							
							save_event_target_as = curse_target
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		
		save_event_target_as = curser
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTA_TIANXIA_2201 # I'll curse them with ugliness!
		
		detract_society_currency_medium_effect = yes
		
		set_character_flag = dw_curse_ugliness
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100 # TIANXIATODO: Logic
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		trigger = {
			event_target:curse_target = {
				NOR = {
					trait = celibate
					AND = {
						is_female = yes
						practical_age >= 45
					}
					trait = eunuch
				}
			}
		}
					
		name = EVTOPTB_TIANXIA_2201 # I'll curse them with infertility!
		
		detract_society_currency_medium_effect = yes
		
		set_character_flag = dw_curse_infertility
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100 # TIANXIATODO: Logic
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTC_TIANXIA_2201 # I'll curse them with misfortune!
		
		detract_society_currency_medium_effect = yes
		
		set_character_flag = dw_curse_misfortune
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100 # TIANXIATODO: Logic
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTD_TIANXIA_2201 # It's too risky!
		
		prestige = -50
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100 # TIANXIATODO: Logic
		}
	}
	
	after = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					has_character_flag = dw_curse_ugliness
					has_character_flag = dw_curse_infertility
					has_character_flag = dw_curse_misfortune
				}
			}
			
			hidden_tooltip = {
				random_list = { # TIANXIATODO: Further logic, including Intrigue checks, suspicion checks, and DW rank checks
					10 = {
						character_event = { id = tianxia.2202 days = 3 random = 3 } # It worked out flawlessly
					}
					20 = {
						character_event = { id = tianxia.2204 days = 3 random = 3 } # Target cursed, but under suspicion
					}
					20 = {
						character_event = { id = tianxia.2216 days = 3 random = 3 } # Target cursed, but caught cursing them
					}
					20 = {
						character_event = { id = tianxia.2228 days = 3 random = 3 } # It didn't work out, but no suspicion
					}
					20 = {
						character_event = { id = tianxia.2229 days = 3 random = 3 } # It didn't work out, and under suspicion
					}
					10 = {
						character_event = { id = tianxia.2241 days = 3 random = 3 } # It didn't work out, and caught cursing them
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# It worked out, not discovered
character_event = {
	id = tianxia.2202
	desc = EVTDESC_TIANXIA_2202
	picture = GFX_evt_garden # TIANXIATODO
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion # TIANXIATODO
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	min_age = 16
	has_dlc = "Mystics"
	is_in_society = yes
	prisoner = no
	
	trigger = {
		is_devil_worshiper_trigger = yes
		is_actually_incapable_trigger = no
		event_target:curse_target = {
			is_alive = yes
			prisoner = no
			is_actually_incapable_trigger = no
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTA_TIANXIA_2202 # Mwahahaha! Etc.
		
		trigger_switch = {
			on_trigger = has_character_flag
			
			dw_curse_ugliness = {
				event_target:curse_target = {
					add_dw_curse_unattractive_modifier_effect = yes
				}
			}
			
			dw_curse_infertility = {
				event_target:curse_target = {
					add_character_modifier = {
						name = dw_curse_infertility
						months = 60
					}
				}
			}
			
			dw_curse_misfortune = {
				event_target:curse_target = {
					add_character_modifier = {
						name = dw_curse_misfortune
						months = 60
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	
	after = {
		event_target:curse_target = {
			character_event = { id = tianxia.2203 } # Target starts feeling the effects of the curse
		}
	}
}

	desc = EVTDESC_TIANXIA_2204

	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion # TIANXIATODO
	
	is_triggered_only = yes

	is_in_society = yes
	prisoner = no

		is_actually_incapable_trigger = no

			is_alive = yes
			prisoner = no
			is_actually_incapable_trigger = no
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTA_TIANXIA_2204 # Could have gone a bit better...
		
		increase_visibility_effect = yes
		
		trigger_switch = {
			on_trigger = has_character_flag
			
			dw_curse_ugliness = {
				event_target:curse_target = {
					add_dw_curse_unattractive_modifier_effect = yes
				}
			}
			
			dw_curse_infertility = {
				event_target:curse_target = {
					add_character_modifier = {
						name = dw_curse_infertility
						months = 60
					}
				}
			}
			
			dw_curse_misfortune = {
				event_target:curse_target = {
					add_character_modifier = {
						name = dw_curse_misfortune
						months = 60
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	
	after = {
		hidden_tooltip = {
			random_list = { # TIANXIATODO: Weights
				50 = {
					event_target:curse_target = {
						character_event = { id = tianxia.2203 } # Target starts feeling the effects of the curse
					}
				}
				50 = {
					event_target:curse_target = {
						character_event = { id = tianxia.2205 } # Target starts feeling the effects of the curse and suspects the curser
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# Target informed - do you wish to accuse the curser?
character_event = {
	id = tianxia.2205
	desc = EVTDESC_TIANXIA_2205
	picture = GFX_evt_garden # TIANXIATODO
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion # TIANXIATODO
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	option = {
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				is_landed = no
				FROM = {
					NOR = {
						AND = {
							is_landed = no
							employer = {
								character = ROOT
							}
						}
						AND = {
							is_landed = yes
							any_liege = {
								character = ROOT
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		
		name = EVTOPTA_TIANXIA_2205 # I've surely been cursed by FROM!
		
		FROM = {
			character_event = { id = tianxia.2206 } # Inform FROM
		}
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100 # TIANXIATODO: Logic
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		trigger = {
			is_landed = yes
			FROM = {
				OR = {
					AND = {
						is_landed = no
						employer = {
							character = ROOT
						}
					}
					AND = {
						is_landed = yes
						any_liege = {
							character = ROOT
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		
		name = EVTOPTB_TIANXIA_2205 # Guards!
		
		FROM = {
			character_event = { id = tianxia.2253 } # Inform FROM
		}
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100 # TIANXIATODO: Logic
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTC_TIANXIA_2205 # I might be imagining things...
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100 # TIANXIATODO: Logic
		}
	}
}

# Curser informed - accused by target
character_event = {
	id = tianxia.2206
	desc = EVTDESC_TIANXIA_2206
	picture = GFX_evt_garden # TIANXIATODO
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion # TIANXIATODO
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	option = {
		trigger = {
			event_target:shared_partner = {
				OR = {
					is_landed = no
					is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = yes
					NOR = {
						AND = {
							is_landed = no
							employer = {
								character = event_target:shared_partner
							}
						}
						AND = {
							is_landed = yes
							any_liege = {
								character = event_target:shared_partner
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		
		name = EVTOPTA_TIANXIA_2206 # You can't prove anything!
	}
	
	option = {
		trigger = {
			event_target:shared_partner = {
				is_landed = yes
				is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
			}
			OR = {
				AND = {
					is_landed = no
					employer = {
						character = event_target:shared_partner
					}
				}
				AND = {
					is_landed = yes
					any_liege = {
						character = event_target:shared_partner
					}
				}
			}
		}
		
		name = EVTOPTB_TIANXIA_2206 # This might be bad...
		
		event_target:shared_partner = {
			character_event = { id = tianxia.2207 } # Fallout of accusation
		}
	}
}

# Fallout - shared spouse decides how to deal with the matter
character_event = {
	id = tianxia.2207
	desc = EVTDESC_TIANXIA_2207
	picture = GFX_evt_garden # TIANXIATODO
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion # TIANXIATODO
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	# TIANXIATODO: Make fairly consistent with the apostate accusation event's logic, though the opinion of the target and the curser should matter
	# TIANXIATODO: Kinslaying penalty
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTA_TIANXIA_2207 # You cursed them? Die!
		
		event_target:curser = {
			tooltip = {
				death = {
					death_reason = death_execution_burning
					killer = ROOT
				}
			}
			
			character_event = { id = tianxia.2208 } # Inform the curser
		}
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100 # TIANXIATODO: Logic
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTB_TIANXIA_2207 # Witchcraft is illegal! Go to jail!
		
		event_target:curser = {
			character_event = { id = tianxia.2210 } # Inform the curser
		}
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100 # TIANXIATODO: Logic
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTC_TIANXIA_2207 # Fetch the the branding irons!
		
		event_target:curser = {
			character_event = { id = tianxia.2212 } # Inform the curser
		}
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100 # TIANXIATODO: Logic
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTD_TIANXIA_2207 # Nonsense! FROM didn't curse FROMFROM!
		
		event_target:curser = {
			character_event = { id = tianxia.2214 } # Inform the curser
		}
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100 # TIANXIATODO: Logic
		}
	}
}

# It worked out, but you got caught
character_event = {
	id = tianxia.2216
	desc = EVTDESC_TIANXIA_2216
	picture = GFX_evt_garden # TIANXIATODO
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion # TIANXIATODO
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	min_age = 16
	has_dlc = "Mystics"
	is_in_society = yes
	prisoner = no
	
	trigger = {
		is_devil_worshiper_trigger = yes
		is_actually_incapable_trigger = no
		event_target:curse_target = {
			is_alive = yes
			prisoner = no
			is_actually_incapable_trigger = no
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTA_TIANXIA_2216 # Not good, even if they're cursed!
		
		increase_visibility_effect = yes # TIANXIATODO: Expose!
		
		trigger_switch = {
			on_trigger = has_character_flag
			
			dw_curse_ugliness = {
				event_target:curse_target = {
					add_dw_curse_unattractive_modifier_effect = yes
				}
			}
			
			dw_curse_infertility = {
				event_target:curse_target = {
					add_character_modifier = {
						name = dw_curse_infertility
						months = 60
					}
				}
			}
			
			dw_curse_misfortune = {
				event_target:curse_target = {
					add_character_modifier = {
						name = dw_curse_misfortune
						months = 60
					}
				}
			}
		}
		
		event_target:curse_target = {
			character_event = { id = tianxia.2217 } # Target starts feeling the effects of the curse and knows who it was
		}
	}
}

# Target informed - you were cursed by FROM
character_event = {
	id = tianxia.2217
	desc = EVTDESC_TIANXIA_2217
	picture = GFX_evt_garden # TIANXIATODO
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion # TIANXIATODO
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	option = {
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				is_landed = no
				FROM = {
					NOR = {
						AND = {
							is_landed = no
							employer = {
								character = ROOT
							}
						}
						AND = {
							is_landed = yes
							any_liege = {
								character = ROOT
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		
		name = EVTOPTA_TIANXIA_2217 # You'll pay for this!
		
		FROM = {
			character_event = { id = tianxia.2218 } # Inform FROM
		}
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100 # TIANXIATODO: Logic
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		trigger = {
			is_landed = yes
			FROM = {
				OR = {
					AND = {
						is_landed = no
						employer = {
							character = ROOT
						}
					}
					AND = {
						is_landed = yes
						any_liege = {
							character = ROOT
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		
		name = EVTOPTB_TIANXIA_2217 # Guards!
		
		FROM = {
			character_event = { id = tianxia.2253 } # Inform FROM
		}
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100 # TIANXIATODO: Logic
		}
	}
}

# Curser informed - accused by target
character_event = {
	id = tianxia.2218
	desc = EVTDESC_TIANXIA_2218
	picture = GFX_evt_garden # TIANXIATODO
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion # TIANXIATODO
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	option = {
		trigger = {
			event_target:shared_partner = {
				OR = {
					is_landed = no
					is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = yes
					NOR = {
						AND = {
							is_landed = no
							employer = {
								character = event_target:shared_partner
							}
						}
						AND = {
							is_landed = yes
							any_liege = {
								character = event_target:shared_partner
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		
		name = EVTOPTA_TIANXIA_2218 # You can't prove anything!
	}
	
	option = {
		trigger = {
			event_target:shared_partner = {
				is_landed = yes
				is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
			}
			OR = {
				AND = {
					is_landed = no
					employer = {
						character = event_target:shared_partner
					}
				}
				AND = {
					is_landed = yes
					any_liege = {
						character = event_target:shared_partner
					}
				}
			}
		}
		
		name = EVTOPTB_TIANXIA_2218 # This might be bad...
		
		event_target:shared_partner = {
			character_event = { id = tianxia.2219 } # Fallout of accusation
		}
	}
}

	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	# TIANXIATODO: Make fairly consistent with the apostate accusation event's logic, though the opinion of the target and the curser should matter

		name = EVTOPTA_TIANXIA_2219 # You cursed them? Die!
		
		event_target:curser = {
			tooltip = {
				death = {
					death_reason = death_execution_burning
					killer = ROOT
				}
			}
			
			character_event = { id = tianxia.2220 } # Inform the curser
		}
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100 # TIANXIATODO: Logic
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTB_TIANXIA_2219 # Witchcraft is illegal! Go to jail!
		
		event_target:curser = {
			character_event = { id = tianxia.2222 } # Inform the curser
		}
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100 # TIANXIATODO: Logic
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTC_TIANXIA_2219 # Fetch the the branding irons!
		
		event_target:curser = {
			character_event = { id = tianxia.2224 } # Inform the curser
		}
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100 # TIANXIATODO: Logic
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTD_TIANXIA_2219 # FROM shouldn't be punished for this!

### Witchcraft - honest suspicion

# You've had some bad luck recently - did another spouse/consort curse you?
character_event = {
	id = tianxia.2261
	desc = EVTDESC_TIANXIA_2261
	picture = GFX_evt_garden # TIANXIATODO
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion # TIANXIATODO
	
	portrait = curser
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	min_age = 16
	prisoner = no
	
	trigger = {
		is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
		OR = {
			AND = {
				is_married = yes
				any_spouse = {
					NOR = {
						is_rival = ROOT
						reverse_has_opinion_modifier = {
							who = ROOT
							name = opinion_abducted
						}
					}
					OR = {
						any_spouse = {
							is_adult = yes
							NOT = {
								character = ROOT
							}
							is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
							NOR = {
								is_friend = ROOT
								is_lover = ROOT
							}
						}
						any_consort = {
							is_adult = yes
							is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
							NOR = {
								is_friend = ROOT
								is_lover = ROOT
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
			AND = {
				is_consort = yes
				is_senior_consort_party = no
				consort = {
					NOR = {
						is_rival = ROOT
						reverse_has_opinion_modifier = {
							who = ROOT
							name = opinion_abducted
						}
					}
					OR = {
						any_spouse = {
							is_adult = yes
							is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
							NOR = {
								is_friend = ROOT
								is_lover = ROOT
							}
						}
						any_consort = {
							is_adult = yes
							NOT = {
								character = ROOT
							}
							is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
							NOR = {
								is_friend = ROOT
								is_lover = ROOT
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	
	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				is_married = yes
			}
			
			random_spouse = {
				limit = {
					NOR = {
						is_rival = ROOT
						reverse_has_opinion_modifier = {
							who = ROOT
							name = opinion_abducted
						}
					}
					OR = {
						any_spouse = {
							is_adult = yes
							NOT = {
								character = ROOT
							}
							is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
							NOR = {
								is_friend = ROOT
								is_lover = ROOT
							}
						}
						any_consort = {
							is_adult = yes
							NOT = {
								character = ROOT
							}
							is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
							NOR = {
								is_friend = ROOT
								is_lover = ROOT
							}
						}
					}
				}
				
				preferred_limit = {
					OR = {
						any_spouse = {
							is_adult = yes
							NOT = {
								character = ROOT
							}
							is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
							is_rival = ROOT
						}
						any_consort = {
							is_adult = yes
							NOT = {
								character = ROOT
							}
							is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
							is_rival = ROOT
						}
					}
				}
				
				save_event_target_as = shared_partner
				
				random_list = {
					50 = {
						trigger = {
							any_spouse = {
								is_adult = yes
								NOT = {
									character = ROOT
								}
								is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
								NOR = {
									is_friend = ROOT
									is_lover = ROOT
								}
							}
						}
						
						random_spouse = {
							limit = {
								is_adult = yes
								NOT = {
									character = ROOT
								}
								is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
								NOR = {
									is_friend = ROOT
									is_lover = ROOT
								}
							}
							
							preferred_limit = {
								is_rival = ROOT
							}
							
							save_event_target_as = curser
						}
					}
					
					50 = {
						trigger = {
							any_consort = {
								is_adult = yes
								NOT = {
									character = ROOT
								}
								is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
								NOR = {
									is_friend = ROOT
									is_lover = ROOT
								}
							}
						}
						
						random_consort = {
							limit = {
								is_adult = yes
								NOT = {
									character = ROOT
								}
								is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
								NOR = {
									is_friend = ROOT
									is_lover = ROOT
								}
							}
							
							preferred_limit = {
								is_rival = ROOT
							}
							
							save_event_target_as = curser
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		
		else = {
			consort = {
				save_event_target_as = shared_partner
				
				random_list = {
					50 = {
						trigger = {
							any_spouse = {
								is_adult = yes
								NOT = {
									character = ROOT
								}
								is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
								NOR = {
									is_friend = ROOT
									is_lover = ROOT
								}
							}
						}
						
						random_spouse = {
							limit = {
								is_adult = yes
								NOT = {
									character = ROOT
								}
								is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
								NOR = {
									is_friend = ROOT
									is_lover = ROOT
								}
							}
							
							preferred_limit = {
								is_rival = ROOT
							}
							
							save_event_target_as = curser
						}
					}
					
					50 = {
						trigger = {
							any_consort = {
								is_adult = yes
								NOT = {
									character = ROOT
								}
								is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
								NOR = {
									is_friend = ROOT
									is_lover = ROOT
								}
							}
						}
						
						random_consort = {
							limit = {
								is_adult = yes
								NOT = {
									character = ROOT
								}
								is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
								NOR = {
									is_friend = ROOT
									is_lover = ROOT
								}
							}
							
							preferred_limit = {
								is_rival = ROOT
							}
							
							save_event_target_as = curser
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		
		save_event_target_as = curse_target
	}
	
	option = {
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				is_landed = no
				event_target:curser = {
					NOR = {
						AND = {
							is_landed = no
							employer = {
								character = ROOT
							}
						}
						AND = {
							is_landed = yes
							any_liege = {
								character = ROOT
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		
		name = EVTOPTA_TIANXIA_2261 # They've surely cursed me!
		
		event_target:curser = {
			character_event = { id = tianxia.2263 } # Inform "curser"
		}
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100 # TIANXIATODO: Logic
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		trigger = {
			is_landed = yes
			event_target:curser = {
				OR = {
					AND = {
						is_landed = no
						employer = {
							character = ROOT
						}
					}
					AND = {
						is_landed = yes
						any_liege = {
							character = ROOT
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		
		event_target:curser = {
			character_event = { id = tianxia.2278 } # Inform "curser"
		}
		
		name = EVTOPTB_TIANXIA_2261 # Guards!
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100 # TIANXIATODO: Logic
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTC_TIANXIA_2261 # I'll have them watched...
		
		event_target:curser = {
			character_event = { id = tianxia.2262 } # Inform "curser"
		}
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100 # TIANXIATODO: Logic
		}
	}

### Witchcraft - fake accusation

# Falsely accuse a fellow spouse/consort of witchcraft targeting you?
character_event = {
	id = tianxia.2286
	desc = EVTDESC_TIANXIA_2286
	picture = GFX_evt_garden # TIANXIATODO
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion # TIANXIATODO
	
	portrait = curser
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	min_age = 16
	prisoner = no
	
	trigger = {
		is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
		OR = {
			AND = {
				is_married = yes
				any_spouse = {
					NOR = {
						is_rival = ROOT
						reverse_has_opinion_modifier = {
							who = ROOT
							name = opinion_abducted
						}
					}
					OR = {
						any_spouse = {
							is_adult = yes
							NOT = {
								character = ROOT
							}
							is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
							NOR = {
								is_friend = ROOT
								is_lover = ROOT
							}
						}
						any_consort = {
							is_adult = yes
							is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
							NOR = {
								is_friend = ROOT
								is_lover = ROOT
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
			AND = {
				is_consort = yes
				is_senior_consort_party = no
				consort = {
					NOR = {
						is_rival = ROOT
						reverse_has_opinion_modifier = {
							who = ROOT
							name = opinion_abducted
						}
					}
					OR = {
						any_spouse = {
							is_adult = yes
							is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
							NOR = {
								is_friend = ROOT
								is_lover = ROOT
							}
						}
						any_consort = {
							is_adult = yes
							NOT = {
								character = ROOT
							}
							is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
							NOR = {
								is_friend = ROOT
								is_lover = ROOT
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	
	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				is_married = yes
			}
			
			random_spouse = {
				limit = {
					NOR = {
						is_rival = ROOT
						reverse_has_opinion_modifier = {
							who = ROOT
							name = opinion_abducted
						}
					}
					OR = {
						any_spouse = {
							is_adult = yes
							NOT = {
								character = ROOT
							}
							is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
							NOR = {
								is_friend = ROOT
								is_lover = ROOT
							}
						}
						any_consort = {
							is_adult = yes
							NOT = {
								character = ROOT
							}
							is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
							NOR = {
								is_friend = ROOT
								is_lover = ROOT
							}
						}
					}
				}
				
				preferred_limit = {
					OR = {
						any_spouse = {
							is_adult = yes
							NOT = {
								character = ROOT
							}
							is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
							is_rival = ROOT
						}
						any_consort = {
							is_adult = yes
							NOT = {
								character = ROOT
							}
							is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
							is_rival = ROOT
						}
					}
				}
				
				save_event_target_as = shared_partner
				
				random_list = {
					50 = {
						trigger = {
							any_spouse = {
								is_adult = yes
								NOT = {
									character = ROOT
								}
								is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
								NOR = {
									is_friend = ROOT
									is_lover = ROOT
								}
							}

							limit = {
								is_adult = yes
								NOT = {
									character = ROOT
								}
								is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
								NOR = {
									is_friend = ROOT
									is_lover = ROOT
								}
							}
							
							preferred_limit = {
								is_rival = ROOT
							}
							
							save_event_target_as = curser
						}
					}
					
					50 = {
						trigger = {
							any_consort = {
								is_adult = yes
								NOT = {
									character = ROOT
								}
								is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
								NOR = {
									is_friend = ROOT
									is_lover = ROOT
								}
							}

									character = ROOT
								}
								is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
								NOR = {
									is_friend = ROOT
									is_lover = ROOT
								}
							}
							
							preferred_limit = {
								is_rival = ROOT
							}
							
							save_event_target_as = curser
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		
		else = {
			consort = {
				save_event_target_as = shared_partner
				
				random_list = {
					50 = {
						trigger = {
							any_spouse = {

							}
							
							save_event_target_as = curser
						}
					}

							any_consort = {
								is_adult = yes
								NOT = {
									character = ROOT
								}
								is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
								NOR = {
									is_friend = ROOT
									is_lover = ROOT
								}
							}
						}

								}

								NOR = {
									is_friend = ROOT

							}
							
							preferred_limit = {

							}
							
							save_event_target_as = curser
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		
		save_event_target_as = curse_target
	}
	
	option = {
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				is_landed = no
				event_target:curser = {
					NOR = {

							}

					}

			}
		}
		
		name = EVTOPTA_TIANXIA_2286 # They've surely cursed me!

### Attempt to scar/disfigure/kill pretty spouse/consort that's more liked than you are

# Prettier spouse/consort is more liked than you are - do something about it?
character_event = {
	id = tianxia.2311
	desc = EVTDESC_TIANXIA_2311
	picture = GFX_evt_garden # TIANXIATODO
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion # TIANXIATODO
	
	is_triggered_only = yes # TIANXIATODO
	
	min_age = 16
	prisoner = no
	
	trigger = {
		is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
		OR = {
			AND = {
				is_married = yes
				any_spouse = {
					NOR = {
						is_rival = ROOT
						reverse_has_opinion_modifier = {
							who = ROOT
							name = opinion_abducted
						}
					}
					valid_romantic_sexual_match_ROOT_only_adult_trigger = yes
					ROOT = {
						valid_romantic_sexual_match_PREV_only_adult_trigger = yes
					}
					OR = {
						any_spouse = {
							is_adult = yes
							is_landed = no
							NOT = {
								character = ROOT
							}
							is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
							NOR = {
								is_friend = ROOT
								is_lover = ROOT
							}
							is_attractive_trigger = yes
							valid_romantic_sexual_match_PREV_only_adult_trigger = yes
							OR = {
								NOR = {
									trait = maimed
									trait = disfigured
								}
								ROOT = {
									combat_rating >= 25
								}
							}
						}
						any_consort = {
							is_adult = yes
							is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
							NOR = {
								is_friend = ROOT
								is_lover = ROOT
							}
							is_attractive_trigger = yes
							valid_romantic_sexual_match_PREV_only_adult_trigger = yes
							OR = {
								NOR = {
									trait = maimed
									trait = disfigured
								}
								ROOT = {
									combat_rating >= 25
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
			AND = {
				is_consort = yes
				is_senior_consort_party = no
				consort = {
					NOR = {
						is_rival = ROOT
						reverse_has_opinion_modifier = {
							who = ROOT

							NOR = {
								is_friend = ROOT
								is_lover = ROOT
							}
							is_attractive_trigger = yes
							valid_romantic_sexual_match_PREV_only_adult_trigger = yes
							OR = {
								NOR = {
									trait = maimed
									trait = disfigured
								}
								ROOT = {
									combat_rating >= 25
								}
							}
						}
						any_consort = {
							is_adult = yes
							NOT = {
								character = ROOT
							}
							is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
							NOR = {
								is_friend = ROOT
								is_lover = ROOT
							}
							is_attractive_trigger = yes
							valid_romantic_sexual_match_PREV_only_adult_trigger = yes
							OR = {
								NOR = {
									trait = maimed
									trait = disfigured
								}
								ROOT = {
									combat_rating >= 25
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		
		OR = {
			trait = cruel
			trait = wroth
			trait = lunatic
			trait = possessed
		}
	}
	
	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				is_married = yes
			}

				limit = {

				limit = {
					is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
					is_landed = yes
					event_target:attacking_consort = {
						OR = {
							is_landed = no
							any_liege = {
								character = event_target:shared_partner
							}
						}
					}
				}
				
				character_event = { id = tianxia.2406 } # Shared spouse gets to deal with it
			}
		}
	}
}

	desc = EVTDESC_TIANXIA_2437
	picture = GFX_evt_garden # TIANXIATODO
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion # TIANXIATODO
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTA_TIANXIA_2437 # Die!
		
		event_target:duel_consort_challenger = {
			tooltip = {
				death = {
					death_reason = death_duel
					killer = ROOT
				}
			}
			character_event = { id = tianxia.2438 } # Inform other party
		}
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100 # TIANXIATODO: Logic
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		trigger = {
			event_target:duel_consort_challenger = {
				is_lover = event_target:shared_partner
			}
		}
		
		name = EVTOPTB_TIANXIA_2437 # You either stop being their lover, or you die!
		
		event_target:duel_consort_challenger = {
			character_event = { id = tianxia.2440 } # Inform other party
		}
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100 # TIANXIATODO: Logic
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		trigger = {
			event_target:duel_consort_challenger = {
				NOR = {
					trait = maimed
					trait = disfigured
				}
			}
		}
		
		name = EVTOPTC_TIANXIA_2437 # Nobody will love you after I disfigure you!
		
		event_target:duel_consort_challenger = {
			tooltip = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_dlc = "Reapers"
					}
					add_trait = disfigured
				}
				else = {
					add_trait_maimed_effect = yes
				}
			}
			character_event = { id = tianxia.2445 } # Inform other party
		}
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100 # TIANXIATODO: Logic
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTD_TIANXIA_2437 # Such a poor fighter!
		
		event_target:duel_consort_challenger = {
			character_event = { id = tianxia.2447 } # Inform other party
		}
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100 # TIANXIATODO: Logic
		}
	}
}

### Siblings are co-spouses/-consorts - getting along

# You're fairly close to a sibling with the same spouse - do you want to try to befriend them?
character_event = {
	id = tianxia.2449
	desc = EVTDESC_TIANXIA_2449
	picture = GFX_evt_garden # TIANXIATODO
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion # TIANXIATODO
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	min_age = 16
	prisoner = no
	
	trigger = {
		is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
		OR = {
			AND = {
				is_married = yes
				any_spouse = {
					OR = {
						any_spouse = {
							is_adult = yes
							NOT = {
								character = ROOT
							}
							sibling = ROOT
							is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
							NOR = {
								is_friend = ROOT
								is_lover = ROOT
								is_rival = ROOT
							}
							opinion = {
								who = ROOT
								value >= 25
							}
							reverse_opinion = {
								who = ROOT
								value >= 25
							}
						}
						any_consort = {
							is_adult = yes
							is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
							NOR = {
								is_friend = ROOT
								is_lover = ROOT
								is_rival = ROOT
							}
							sibling = ROOT
							opinion = {
								who = ROOT
								value >= 25
							}
							reverse_opinion = {
								who = ROOT
								value >= 25
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
			AND = {
				is_consort = yes
				is_senior_consort_party = no
				consort = {
					OR = {
						any_spouse = {
							is_adult = yes
							sibling = ROOT
							is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
							NOR = {
								is_friend = ROOT
								is_lover = ROOT
								is_rival = ROOT
							}
							opinion = {
								who = ROOT
								value >= 25
							}
							reverse_opinion = {
								who = ROOT
								value >= 25
							}
						}
						any_consort = {
							is_adult = yes
							NOT = {
								character = ROOT
							}
							sibling = ROOT
							is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
							NOR = {
								is_friend = ROOT
								is_lover = ROOT
								is_rival = ROOT
							}
							opinion = {
								who = ROOT
								value >= 25
							}
							reverse_opinion = {
								who = ROOT
								value >= 25
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	
	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				is_married = yes
			}
			
			random_spouse = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						any_spouse = {
							is_adult = yes
							NOT = {
								character = ROOT
							}
							sibling = ROOT
							is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
							NOR = {
								is_friend = ROOT
								is_lover = ROOT
								is_rival = ROOT
							}
							opinion = {
								who = ROOT
								value >= 25
							}
							reverse_opinion = {
								who = ROOT
								value >= 25
							}
						}
						any_consort = {
							is_adult = yes
							NOT = {
								character = ROOT
							}
							sibling = ROOT
							is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
							NOR = {
								is_friend = ROOT
								is_lover = ROOT
								is_rival = ROOT
							}
							opinion = {
								who = ROOT
								value >= 25
							}
							reverse_opinion = {
								who = ROOT
								value >= 25
							}
						}
					}
				}
				
				save_event_target_as = shared_partner
				
				random_list = {
					50 = {
						trigger = {
							any_spouse = {
								is_adult = yes
								NOT = {
									character = ROOT
								}
								sibling = ROOT
								is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
								NOR = {
									is_friend = ROOT
									is_lover = ROOT
									is_rival = ROOT
								}
								opinion = {
									who = ROOT
									value >= 25
								}
								reverse_opinion = {
									who = ROOT
									value >= 25
								}
							}
						}
						
						random_spouse = {
							limit = {
								is_adult = yes
								NOT = {
									character = ROOT
								}
								sibling = ROOT
								is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
								NOR = {
									is_friend = ROOT
									is_lover = ROOT
									is_rival = ROOT
								}
								opinion = {
									who = ROOT
									value >= 25
								}

						is_lover = event_target:sibling_consort

					NOT = {

					}

					is_lover = event_target:focus_consort

					is_friend = event_target:sibling_consort

				}
			}
		}

						is_lover = event_target:focus_consort
					}
					is_rival = event_target:sibling_consort
				}

						is_friend = event_target:sibling_consort
						is_lover = event_target:sibling_consort
					}
					is_rival = event_target:focus_consort
				}
			}
		}

### Siblings are co-spouses/-consorts - not getting along

# You rather dislike a sibling with the same spouse - do you consider them a rival?
character_event = {
	id = tianxia.2463
	desc = EVTDESC_TIANXIA_2463
	picture = GFX_evt_garden # TIANXIATODO
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion # TIANXIATODO
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	min_age = 16
	prisoner = no
	
	trigger = {
		is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
		OR = {
			AND = {
				is_married = yes
				any_spouse = {
					OR = {
						any_spouse = {
							is_adult = yes
							NOT = {
								character = ROOT
							}
							sibling = ROOT
							is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
							NOR = {
								is_friend = ROOT
								is_lover = ROOT
								is_rival = ROOT
							}
							opinion = {
								who = ROOT
								value <= -25
							}
							reverse_opinion = {
								who = ROOT
								value <= -25
							}
						}
						any_consort = {
							is_adult = yes
							sibling = ROOT
							is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
							NOR = {
								is_friend = ROOT
								is_lover = ROOT
								is_rival = ROOT
							}
							opinion = {
								who = ROOT
								value <= -25
							}
							reverse_opinion = {
								who = ROOT
								value <= -25
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
			AND = {
				is_consort = yes
				is_senior_consort_party = no
				consort = {
					OR = {
						any_spouse = {
							is_adult = yes
							sibling = ROOT
							is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
							NOR = {
								is_friend = ROOT
								is_lover = ROOT
								is_rival = ROOT
							}
							opinion = {
								who = ROOT
								value <= -25
							}
							reverse_opinion = {
								who = ROOT
								value <= -25
							}
						}
						any_consort = {
							is_adult = yes
							NOT = {
								character = ROOT
							}
							sibling = ROOT
							is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
							NOR = {
								is_friend = ROOT
								is_lover = ROOT

# Shared spouse informed of rivalry
character_event = {
	id = tianxia.2465
	desc = EVTDESC_TIANXIA_2465
	picture = GFX_evt_garden # TIANXIATODO
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion # TIANXIATODO
	
	is_triggered_only = yes # TIANXIATODO
	
	option = {
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				is_friend = event_target:focus_consort
				is_lover = event_target:focus_consort
			}
			OR = {
				is_friend = event_target:sibling_consort
				is_lover = event_target:sibling_consort
			}
		}
		
		name = EVTOPTA_TIANXIA_2465 # That's unfortunate...
	}
	
	option = {
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				AND = {
					OR = {
						is_friend = event_target:focus_consort
						is_lover = event_target:focus_consort
					}
					NOT = {
						is_rival = event_target:sibling_consort
					}
				}
				AND = {
					OR = {
						is_friend = event_target:sibling_consort
						is_lover = event_target:sibling_consort
					}
					NOT = {
						is_rival = event_target:focus_consort
					}
				}
			}
			NAND = {
				OR = {
					is_friend = event_target:focus_consort
					is_lover = event_target:focus_consort
				}
				OR = {
					is_friend = event_target:sibling_consort
					is_lover = event_target:sibling_consort
				}
			}
		}
		
		name = EVTOPTB_TIANXIA_2465 # Not ideal...
	}
	
	option = {
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				AND = {
					OR = {
						is_friend = event_target:focus_consort
						is_lover = event_target:focus_consort
					}
					is_rival = event_target:sibling_consort
				}
				AND = {
					OR = {
						is_friend = event_target:sibling_consort
						is_lover = event_target:sibling_consort
					}
					is_rival = event_target:focus_consort
				}
			}
		}
		
		name = EVTOPTC_TIANXIA_2465 # I can certainly see that!
	}
	
	option = {
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				is_rival = event_target:sibling_consort
				is_rival = event_target:focus_consort
			}
			NOR = {
				AND = {
					is_rival = event_target:sibling_consort
					is_rival = event_target:focus_consort
				}
				is_friend = event_target:focus_consort
				is_lover = event_target:focus_consort
				is_friend = event_target:sibling_consort
				is_lover = event_target:sibling_consort
			}
		}
		
		name = EVTOPTD_TIANXIA_2465 # Might be a good thing!
	}
	
	option = {
		trigger = {
			is_rival = event_target:sibling_consort
			is_rival = event_target:focus_consort
		}
		
		name = EVTOPTE_TIANXIA_2465 # I hope they both suffer!
	}
}

### Revenge on rival spouse/consort after succession if heir is your child

# on_death event for spouse to set it up
character_event = {
	id = tianxia.2489
	hide_window = yes
	
	is_triggered_only = yes # on_death
	
	trigger = {
		higher_real_tier_than = count
		
		OR = {
			any_spouse = {
				is_adult = yes
				is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
				ROOT = {
					player_heir = {
						is_child_of = PREVPREV
						is_primary_heir = ROOT
					}
					OR = {
						any_spouse = {
							is_landed = no
							is_adult = yes
							is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
							NOT = {
								character = PREVPREV
							}
							OR = {
								is_rival = PREVPREV
								AND = {
									OR = {
										is_lover = ROOT
										is_attractive_trigger = yes
									}
									NOR = {
										is_lover = PREVPREV
										is_friend = PREVPREV
									}
									PREVPREV = {
										trait = envious
									}
								}
							}
						}
						any_consort = {
							is_landed = no
							is_adult = yes
							is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
							NOT = {
								character = PREVPREV
							}
							OR = {
								is_rival = PREVPREV
								AND = {
									OR = {
										is_lover = ROOT
										is_attractive_trigger = yes
									}
									NOR = {
										is_lover = PREVPREV
										is_friend = PREVPREV
									}
									PREVPREV = {
										trait = envious
									}
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
				NOT = {
					trait = kind
				}
				OR = {
					trait = wroth
					trait = cruel
				}
			}
			any_consort = {
				is_adult = yes
				is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
				ROOT = {
					player_heir = {
						is_child_of = PREVPREV
						is_primary_heir = ROOT
					}
					OR = {
						any_spouse = {
							is_landed = no
							is_adult = yes
							is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
							NOT = {
								character = PREVPREV
							}
							OR = {
								is_rival = PREVPREV
								AND = {
									OR = {
										is_lover = ROOT
										is_attractive_trigger = yes
									}
									NOR = {
										is_lover = PREVPREV
										is_friend = PREVPREV
									}
									PREVPREV = {
										trait = envious
									}
								}
							}
						}
						any_consort = {
							is_landed = no
							is_adult = yes
							is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
							NOT = {
								character = PREVPREV
							}
							OR = {
								is_rival = PREVPREV
								AND = {
									OR = {
										is_lover = ROOT
										is_attractive_trigger = yes
									}
									NOR = {
										is_lover = PREVPREV
										is_friend = PREVPREV
									}
									PREVPREV = {
										trait = envious
									}
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
				NOT = {
					trait = kind
				}
				OR = {
					trait = wroth
					trait = cruel
				}
			}
		}
	}
	
	immediate = {
		save_event_target_as = dying_ruler
		
		player_heir = {
			save_event_target_as = heir_of_dying_ruler
			
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = {
						mother = {
							character = ROOT
						}
					}
				}
				
				mother = {
					save_event_target_as = revenge_spouse
				}
			}
			
			else = {
				father = {
					save_event_target_as = revenge_spouse
				}
			}
		}
		
		random_list = {
			50 = {
				trigger = {
					any_spouse = {
						is_landed = no
						is_adult = yes
						is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
						NOT = {
							character = event_target:revenge_spouse
						}
						OR = {
							is_rival = event_target:revenge_spouse
							AND = {
								OR = {
									is_lover = ROOT
									is_attractive_trigger = yes
								}
								NOR = {
									is_lover = event_target:revenge_spouse
									is_friend = event_target:revenge_spouse
								}
								event_target:revenge_spouse = {
									trait = envious
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
				
				random_spouse = {
					limit = {
						is_landed = no
						is_adult = yes
						is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
						NOT = {
							character = event_target:revenge_spouse
						}
						OR = {
							is_rival = event_target:revenge_spouse
							AND = {
								OR = {
									is_lover = ROOT
									is_attractive_trigger = yes
								}
								NOR = {
									is_lover = event_target:revenge_spouse
									is_friend = event_target:revenge_spouse
								}
								event_target:revenge_spouse = {
									trait = envious
								}
							}
						}
					}
					
					preferred_limit = {
						is_rival = event_target:revenge_spouse
						is_actually_incapable_trigger = yes
						is_lover = ROOT
						NOR = {
							is_lover = event_target:revenge_spouse
							is_friend = event_target:revenge_spouse
						}
						event_target:revenge_spouse = {
							trait = envious
						}
					}
					
					preferred_limit = {
						is_rival = event_target:revenge_spouse
					}
					
					preferred_limit = {
						is_lover = ROOT
						NOR = {
							is_lover = event_target:revenge_spouse
							is_friend = event_target:revenge_spouse
						}
						event_target:revenge_spouse = {
							trait = envious
						}
					}
					
					save_event_target_as = rival_spouse
				}
			}
			50 = {
				trigger = {
					any_consort = {
						is_landed = no
						is_adult = yes
						is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
						NOT = {
							character = event_target:revenge_spouse
						}
						OR = {
							is_rival = event_target:revenge_spouse
							AND = {
								OR = {
									is_lover = ROOT
									is_attractive_trigger = yes
								}
								NOR = {
									is_lover = event_target:revenge_spouse
									is_friend = event_target:revenge_spouse
								}
								event_target:revenge_spouse = {
									trait = envious
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
				
				random_consort = {
					limit = {
						is_landed = no
						is_adult = yes
						is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
						NOT = {
							character = event_target:revenge_spouse
						}
						OR = {
							is_rival = event_target:revenge_spouse
							AND = {
								OR = {
									is_lover = ROOT
									is_attractive_trigger = yes
								}
								NOR = {
									is_lover = event_target:revenge_spouse
									is_friend = event_target:revenge_spouse
								}
								event_target:revenge_spouse = {
									trait = envious
								}
							}
						}
					}
					
					preferred_limit = {
						is_rival = event_target:revenge_spouse
						is_actually_incapable_trigger = yes
						is_lover = ROOT
						NOR = {
							is_lover = event_target:revenge_spouse
							is_friend = event_target:revenge_spouse
						}
						event_target:revenge_spouse = {
							trait = envious
						}
					}
					
					preferred_limit = {
						is_rival = event_target:revenge_spouse
					}
					
					preferred_limit = {
						is_lover = ROOT
						NOR = {
							is_lover = event_target:revenge_spouse
							is_friend = event_target:revenge_spouse
						}
						event_target:revenge_spouse = {
							trait = envious
						}
					}
					
					save_event_target_as = rival_spouse
				}
			}
		}
		
		event_target:revenge_spouse = {
			character_event = { id = tianxia.2490 days = 2 }
		}
	}
}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTA_TIANXIA_2505 # They will die!
			
		hidden_tooltip = {
			random_list = { # TIANXIATODO: Further logic
				25 = {
					character_event = { id = tianxia.2506 days = 3 random = 3 } # It worked out flawlessly
				}
				25 = {
					character_event = { id = tianxia.2509 days = 3 random = 3 } # Target died, but you were discovered
				}
				25 = {
					character_event = { id = tianxia.2515 days = 3 random = 3 } # Target drank poison but lived, not discovered
				}
				25 = {
					character_event = { id = tianxia.2518 days = 3 random = 3 } # It didn't work out, and you were caught!
				}
			}
		}
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100 # TIANXIATODO: Logic
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTB_TIANXIA_2505 # It's too risky!
		
		prestige = -50
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100 # TIANXIATODO: Logic

### Wife wants to be primary wife

# Do you want to try to supplant the primary wife?
character_event = {
	id = tianxia.2527
	desc = EVTDESC_TIANXIA_2527
	picture = GFX_evt_garden # TIANXIATODO
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion # TIANXIATODO
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	only_women = yes
	min_age = 16
	prisoner = no
	
	trigger = {
		is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
		is_married = yes
		is_main_spouse = no
		spouse = {
			is_landed = yes
			NOR = {
				is_rival = ROOT
				reverse_has_opinion_modifier = {
					who = ROOT
					name = opinion_abducted
				}
			}
			is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
			spouse = {
				OR = {
					is_rival = ROOT
					AND = {
						NOR = {
							is_friend = ROOT
							is_lover = ROOT
						}
						OR = {
							reverse_opinion = {
								who = ROOT
								value < -25
							}
							trait = envious
							trait = ambitious
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	
	immediate = {
		save_event_target_as = wants_to_be_primary_spouse
		
		spouse = {
			save_event_target_as = shared_partner
			
			spouse = {
				save_event_target_as = primary_spouse
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTA_TIANXIA_2527 # I'll ask them!
		
		event_target:primary_spouse = {
			character_event = { id = tianxia.2528 } # Request made
		}
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100 # TIANXIATODO: Logic
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTB_TIANXIA_2527 # I don't want to do that...
		
		prestige = -50
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100 # TIANXIATODO: Logic
		}
	}
}

# Spouse informed - what do?
character_event = {
	id = tianxia.2528
	desc = EVTDESC_TIANXIA_2528
	picture = GFX_evt_garden # TIANXIATODO
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion # TIANXIATODO
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOR = {
					is_lover = event_target:wants_to_be_primary_spouse
					is_lover = event_target:primary_spouse
				}
			}
			
			random_spouse = {
				limit = {
					NOR = {
						character = event_target:wants_to_be_primary_spouse
						character = event_target:primary_spouse
						is_rival = ROOT
						has_opinion_modifier = {
							who = ROOT
							name = opinion_abducted
						}
					}
					
					OR = {
						is_lover = ROOT
						AND = {
							is_friend = ROOT
							ROOT = {
								NOR = {
									is_friend = event_target:wants_to_be_primary_spouse
									is_friend = event_target:primary_spouse
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
				
				preferred_limit = {
					is_lover = ROOT
					reverse_opinion = {
						who = ROOT
						value >= 75
					}
				}
				
				preferred_limit = {
					is_lover = ROOT
					reverse_opinion = {
						who = ROOT
						value >= 50
					}
				}
				
				preferred_limit = {
					is_lover = ROOT
					reverse_opinion = {
						who = ROOT
						value >= 25
					}
				}
				
				preferred_limit = {
					reverse_opinion = {
						who = ROOT
						value >= 75
					}
				}
				
				preferred_limit = {
					reverse_opinion = {
						who = ROOT
						value >= 50
					}
				}
				
				preferred_limit = {
					reverse_opinion = {
						who = ROOT
						value >= 25
					}
				}
				
				save_event_target_as = other_spouse_option
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTA_TIANXIA_2528 # An excellent suggestion!
		
		event_target:wants_to_be_primary_spouse = {
			make_primary_spouse = yes
			
			character_event = { id = tianxia.2529 } # Inform the new primary wife
		}
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100 # TIANXIATODO: Logic
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTB_TIANXIA_2528 # I think not!
		
		event_target:wants_to_be_primary_spouse = {
			character_event = { id = tianxia.2537 } # Inform the instigator
		}
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100 # TIANXIATODO: Logic
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		trigger = {
			event_target:other_spouse_option = {
				always = yes
			}
		}
		
		name = EVTOPTC_TIANXIA_2528 # Oh, I *do* think I will be getting a new primary spouse...
		
		event_target:wants_to_be_primary_spouse = {
			character_event = { id = tianxia.2544 } # Inform the instigator
		}
		
		event_target:primary_spouse = {
			character_event = { id = tianxia.2546 } # Inform the former primary wife
		}
		
		event_target:other_spouse_option = {
			make_primary_spouse = yes
			
			character_event = { id = tianxia.2548 } # Inform new primary wife
		}
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100 # TIANXIATODO: Logic
		}
	}
}

# Inform former primary wife - replaced - reaction towards instigator
character_event = {
	id = tianxia.2533
	desc = EVTDESC_TIANXIA_2533
	picture = GFX_evt_garden # TIANXIATODO
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion # TIANXIATODO
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	option = {
		trigger = {
			is_lover = event_target:wants_to_be_primary_spouse
		}
		
		name = EVTOPTA_TIANXIA_2533 # Clearly, she never loved me!
		
		opinion = {
			who = event_target:wants_to_be_primary_spouse
			modifier = opinion_replaced_me
			years = 10
		}
		
		event_target:wants_to_be_primary_spouse = {
			character_event = { id = tianxia.2534 } # Inform new primary wife
		}
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100 # TIANXIATODO: Logic
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				is_lover = event_target:wants_to_be_primary_spouse
			}
			is_friend = event_target:wants_to_be_primary_spouse
		}
		
		name = EVTOPTB_TIANXIA_2533 # Clearly, she's not my friend!
		
		opinion = {
			who = event_target:wants_to_be_primary_spouse
			modifier = opinion_replaced_me
			years = 10
		}
		
		event_target:wants_to_be_primary_spouse = {
			character_event = { id = tianxia.2535 } # Inform primary wife
		}
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100 # TIANXIATODO: Logic
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		trigger = {
			NOR = {
				is_lover = event_target:wants_to_be_primary_spouse
				is_friend = event_target:wants_to_be_primary_spouse
				is_rival = event_target:wants_to_be_primary_spouse
			}
		}
		
		name = EVTOPTC_TIANXIA_2533 # She's going down!
		
		add_rival = event_target:wants_to_be_primary_spouse
		
		opinion = {
			who = event_target:wants_to_be_primary_spouse
			modifier = opinion_replaced_me
			years = 10
		}
		
		event_target:wants_to_be_primary_spouse = {
			character_event = { id = tianxia.2536 } # Inform primary wife
		}
		
		opinion = {
			who = event_target:wants_to_be_primary_spouse
			modifier = opinion_demoted
			years = 10
		}
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100 # TIANXIATODO: Logic
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		trigger = {
			is_rival = event_target:wants_to_be_primary_spouse
		}
		
		name = EVTOPTD_TIANXIA_2533 # That witch with a capital B!
		
		opinion = {
			who = event_target:wants_to_be_primary_spouse
			modifier = opinion_replaced_me
			years = 10
		}
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100 # TIANXIATODO: Logic
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				is_rival = event_target:wants_to_be_primary_spouse
			}
		}
		
		name = EVTOPTE_TIANXIA_2533 # It's fine!
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100 # TIANXIATODO: Logic
		}
	}
}

	id = tianxia.2537
	desc = EVTDESC_TIANXIA_2537
	picture = GFX_evt_garden # TIANXIATODO
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion # TIANXIATODO
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	option = {
		trigger = {
			is_lover = event_target:shared_partner
		}
		
		name = EVTOPTA_TIANXIA_2537 # Clearly, he never loved me!
		
		opinion = {
			who = event_target:shared_partner
			modifier = opinion_did_not_promote_me
			years = 10
		}
		
		event_target:shared_partner = {
			character_event = { id = tianxia.2538 } # Inform spouse
		}
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100 # TIANXIATODO: Logic
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				is_lover = event_target:shared_partner
			}
			is_friend = event_target:shared_partner
		}
		
		name = EVTOPTB_TIANXIA_2537 # Clearly, he's not my friend!
		
		opinion = {
			who = event_target:shared_partner
			modifier = opinion_did_not_promote_me
			years = 10
		}
		
		event_target:shared_partner = {
			character_event = { id = tianxia.2539 } # Inform spouse
		}
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100 # TIANXIATODO: Logic
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		trigger = {
			NOR = {
				is_lover = event_target:shared_partner
				is_friend = event_target:shared_partner
			}
		}
		
		name = EVTOPTC_TIANXIA_2537 # I deserve better!
		
		opinion = {
			who = event_target:shared_partner
			modifier = opinion_did_not_promote_me
			years = 10
		}
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100 # TIANXIATODO: Logic
		}
	}
	
	after = {
		character_event = { id = tianxia.2540 }
	}
}

### Concubine/consorts wants to be spouse

# Do you want to try to supplant the spouse?
character_event = {
	id = tianxia.2557
	desc = EVTDESC_TIANXIA_2557
	picture = GFX_evt_garden # TIANXIATODO
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion # TIANXIATODO
	
	is_triggered_only = yes # TIANXIATODO
	
	min_age = 16
	prisoner = no
	
	trigger = {
		is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
		is_consort = yes
		is_senior_consort_party = no
		consort = {
			is_landed = yes
			NOR = {
				is_rival = ROOT
				reverse_has_opinion_modifier = {
					who = ROOT
					name = opinion_abducted
				}
			}
			is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
			spouse = {
				is_landed = no # Can't become a concubine/consort if landed...
				OR = {
					is_rival = ROOT
					AND = {
						NOR = {
							is_friend = ROOT
							is_lover = ROOT
						}
						OR = {
							reverse_opinion = {
								who = ROOT
								value < -25
							}
							trait = envious
							trait = ambitious
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	
	immediate = {
		save_event_target_as = wants_to_be_primary_spouse
		
		consort = {
			save_event_target_as = shared_partner
			
			spouse = {
				save_event_target_as = primary_spouse
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTA_TIANXIA_2557 # I'll ask them!
		
		event_target:primary_spouse = {
			character_event = { id = tianxia.2558 } # Request made
		}
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100 # TIANXIATODO: Logic
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTB_TIANXIA_2557 # I don't want to do that...
		
		prestige = -50
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100 # TIANXIATODO: Logic
		}
	}
}

# Spouse informed - what do?
character_event = {
	id = tianxia.2558
	desc = EVTDESC_TIANXIA_2558
	picture = GFX_evt_garden # TIANXIATODO
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion # TIANXIATODO
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOR = {
					is_lover = event_target:wants_to_be_primary_spouse
					is_lover = event_target:primary_spouse
				}
			}
			
			random_consort = {
				limit = {
					NOR = {
						character = event_target:wants_to_be_primary_spouse
						character = event_target:primary_spouse
						is_rival = ROOT
						has_opinion_modifier = {
							who = ROOT
							name = opinion_abducted
						}
					}
					
					OR = {
						is_lover = ROOT
						AND = {
							is_friend = ROOT
							ROOT = {
								NOR = {
									is_friend = event_target:wants_to_be_primary_spouse
									is_friend = event_target:primary_spouse
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
				
				preferred_limit = {
					is_lover = ROOT
					reverse_opinion = {
						who = ROOT
						value >= 75
					}
				}
				
				preferred_limit = {
					is_lover = ROOT
					reverse_opinion = {
						who = ROOT
						value >= 50
					}
				}
				
				preferred_limit = {
					is_lover = ROOT
					reverse_opinion = {
						who = ROOT
						value >= 25
					}
				}
				
				preferred_limit = {
					reverse_opinion = {
						who = ROOT
						value >= 75
					}
				}
				
				preferred_limit = {
					reverse_opinion = {
						who = ROOT
						value >= 50
					}
				}
				
				preferred_limit = {
					reverse_opinion = {
						who = ROOT
						value >= 25
					}
				}
				
				save_event_target_as = other_spouse_option
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTA_TIANXIA_2558 # An excellent suggestion!
		
		event_target:primary_spouse = {
			remove_spouse = ROOT
			
			ROOT = {
				add_consort = PREV
			}
		}
		
		event_target:wants_to_be_primary_spouse = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					is_female = yes
				}
				
				add_spouse = ROOT
			}
			
			else = {
				add_spouse_matrilineal = ROOT
			}
			
			character_event = { id = tianxia.2559 } # Inform the new spouse
		}
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100 # TIANXIATODO: Logic
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTB_TIANXIA_2558 # I think not!
		
		event_target:wants_to_be_primary_spouse = {
			character_event = { id = tianxia.2567 } # Inform the instigator
		}
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100 # TIANXIATODO: Logic
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		trigger = {
			event_target:other_spouse_option = {
				always = yes
			}
		}
		
		name = EVTOPTC_TIANXIA_2558 # Oh, I *do* think I will be getting a new spouse...
		
		event_target:wants_to_be_primary_spouse = {
			character_event = { id = tianxia.2574 } # Inform the instigator
		}
		
		event_target:primary_spouse = {
			remove_spouse = ROOT
			
			ROOT = {
				add_consort = PREV
			}
			
			character_event = { id = tianxia.2576 } # Inform the former spouse
		}
		
		event_target:other_spouse_option = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					is_female = yes
				}
				
				add_spouse = ROOT
			}
			
			else = {
				add_spouse_matrilineal = ROOT
			}
			
			character_event = { id = tianxia.2578 } # Inform new spouse
		}
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100 # TIANXIATODO: Logic
		}
	}
}

# Reaction to new spouse - instigator
character_event = {
	id = tianxia.2579
	desc = EVTDESC_TIANXIA_2579
	picture = GFX_evt_garden # TIANXIATODO
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion # TIANXIATODO
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	option = {
		trigger = {
			is_lover = event_target:other_spouse_option
		}
		
		name = EVTOPTA_TIANXIA_2579 # I hate them!
		
		remove_lover = event_target:other_spouse_option
		
		opinion = {
			who = event_target:other_spouse_option
			modifier = opinion_in_my_way
			years = 10
		}
		
		event_target:other_spouse_option = {
			character_event = { id = tianxia.2580 } # Inform new spouse
		}
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100 # TIANXIATODO: Logic
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				is_lover = event_target:other_spouse_option
			}
			is_friend = event_target:other_spouse_option
		}
		
		remove_friend = event_target:other_spouse_option
		
		name = EVTOPTB_TIANXIA_2579 # I hate them!
		
		opinion = {
			who = event_target:other_spouse_option
			modifier = opinion_in_my_way
			years = 10
		}
		
		event_target:other_spouse_option = {
			character_event = { id = tianxia.2581 } # Inform new spouse
		}
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100 # TIANXIATODO: Logic
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		trigger = {
			NOR = {
				is_lover = event_target:other_spouse_option
				is_friend = event_target:other_spouse_option
				is_rival = event_target:other_spouse_option
			}
		}
		
		name = EVTOPTC_TIANXIA_2579 # I hate them!
		
		add_rival = event_target:other_spouse_option
		
		opinion = {
			who = event_target:other_spouse_option
			modifier = opinion_in_my_way
			years = 10
		}
		
		event_target:other_spouse_option = {
			character_event = { id = tianxia.2582 } # Inform new spouse
		}
		
		opinion = {
			who = event_target:shared_partner
			modifier = opinion_demoted
			years = 10
		}
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100 # TIANXIATODO: Logic
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		trigger = {
			is_rival = event_target:other_spouse_option
		}
		
		name = EVTOPTD_TIANXIA_2579 # They're in my way, as always!
		
		opinion = {
			who = event_target:other_spouse_option
			modifier = opinion_in_my_way
			years = 10
		}
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100 # TIANXIATODO: Logic
		}
	}
}

# Other spouse/consort and you get along very well - is it love?
character_event = {
	id = tianxia.2587
	desc = EVTDESC_TIANXIA_2587
	picture = GFX_evt_garden # TIANXIATODO
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion # TIANXIATODO
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	min_age = 16
	prisoner = no
	
	trigger = {
		is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
		OR = {
			any_spouse = {
				OR = {
					any_spouse = {
						is_adult = yes
						is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
						NOR = {
							character = ROOT
							is_lover = ROOT
							is_rival = ROOT
						}
						valid_romantic_sexual_match_ROOT_only_adult_trigger = yes
						ROOT = {
							valid_romantic_sexual_match_PREV_only_adult_trigger = yes
						}
						opinion = {
							who = ROOT
							value >= 25
						}
						reverse_opinion = {
							who = ROOT
							value >= 25
						}
					}
					any_consort = {
						is_adult = yes
						is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no

							character = ROOT
							is_lover = ROOT

# Friendship formed - shared spouse
character_event = {
	id = tianxia.2592
	desc = EVTDESC_TIANXIA_2592
	picture = GFX_evt_garden # TIANXIATODO
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion # TIANXIATODO
	
	is_triggered_only = yes # TIANXIATODO
	
	option = {
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				is_friend = event_target:lover_consort
				is_lover = event_target:lover_consort
			}
			OR = {
				is_friend = event_target:possible_lover
				is_lover = event_target:possible_lover
			}
		}
		
		name = EVTOPTA_TIANXIA_2592 # Most excellent!
	}
	
	option = {
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				AND = {
					OR = {
						is_friend = event_target:lover_consort
						is_lover = event_target:lover_consort
					}
					NOT = {
						is_rival = event_target:possible_lover
					}
				}
				AND = {
					OR = {
						is_friend = event_target:possible_lover
						is_lover = event_target:possible_lover
					}
					NOT = {
						is_rival = event_target:lover_consort
					}
				}
			}
			NAND = {
				OR = {
					is_friend = event_target:lover_consort
					is_lover = event_target:lover_consort
				}
				OR = {
					is_friend = event_target:possible_lover
					is_lover = event_target:possible_lover
				}
			}
		}
		
		name = EVTOPTB_TIANXIA_2592 # I'm sure that's good...
	}
	
	option = {
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				AND = {
					OR = {
						is_friend = event_target:lover_consort
						is_lover = event_target:lover_consort
					}
					is_rival = event_target:possible_lover
				}
				AND = {
					OR = {
						is_friend = event_target:possible_lover
						is_lover = event_target:possible_lover
					}
					is_rival = event_target:lover_consort
				}
			}
		}
		
		name = EVTOPTC_TIANXIA_2592 # A strange friendship...
	}
	
	option = {
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				is_rival = event_target:possible_lover
				is_rival = event_target:lover_consort
			}
			NOR = {
				AND = {
					is_rival = event_target:possible_lover
					is_rival = event_target:lover_consort
				}
				is_friend = event_target:lover_consort
				is_lover = event_target:lover_consort
				is_friend = event_target:possible_lover
				is_lover = event_target:possible_lover
			}
		}
		
		name = EVTOPTD_TIANXIA_2592 # Might be bad news...
	}
	
	option = {
		trigger = {
			is_rival = event_target:possible_lover
			is_rival = event_target:lover_consort
		}
		
		name = EVTOPTE_TIANXIA_2592 # I have a bad feeling about this...
	}
}

# Shared spouse finds out about love affair - can fire from various places; TIANXIATODO: Hook in
character_event = {
	id = tianxia.2598
	desc = EVTDESC_TIANXIA_2598
	picture = GFX_evt_garden # TIANXIATODO
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion # TIANXIATODO
	
	is_triggered_only = yes # TIANXIATODO
	
	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				is_lover = event_target:lover_consort_one
			}
			
			event_target:lover_consort_one = {
				save_event_target_as = my_lover
			}
		}
		
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				is_lover = event_target:lover_consort_two
			}
			
			event_target:lover_consort_two = {
				save_event_target_as = my_lover
			}
		}
		
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					is_lover = event_target:lover_consort_one
				}
			}
			
			event_target:lover_consort_one = {
				save_event_target_as = not_lover
			}
		}
		
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					is_lover = event_target:lover_consort_two
				}
			}
			
			event_target:lover_consort_two = {
				save_event_target_as = not_lover
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		trigger = {
			is_lover = event_target:lover_consort_one
			is_lover = event_target:lover_consort_two
		}
		
		name = EVTOPTA_TIANXIA_2598 # This can only mean good things!
		
		event_target:lover_consort_one = {
			character_event = { id = tianxia.2599 } # Inform lovers
		}
		
		event_target:lover_consort_two = {
			character_event = { id = tianxia.2599 } # Inform lovers
		}
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100 # TIANXIATODO: Logic
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				AND = {
					is_lover = event_target:lover_consort_one
					event_target:lover_consort_two = {
						NOT = {
							is_lover = ROOT
						}
						valid_romantic_sexual_match_ROOT_only_adult_trigger = yes
						ROOT = {
							valid_romantic_sexual_match_ROOT_only_adult_trigger = yes
						}
					}
				}
				AND = {
					is_lover = event_target:lover_consort_two
					event_target:lover_consort_one = {
						NOT = {
							is_lover = ROOT
						}
						valid_romantic_sexual_match_ROOT_only_adult_trigger = yes
						ROOT = {
							valid_romantic_sexual_match_ROOT_only_adult_trigger = yes
						}
					}
				}
			}
			NOR = {
				is_rival = event_target:lover_consort_one
				is_rival = event_target:lover_consort_two
				has_opinion_modifier = {
					who = event_target:lover_consort_one
					modifier = opinion_abducted
				}
				has_opinion_modifier = {
					who = event_target:lover_consort_two
					modifier = opinion_abducted
				}
				reverse_has_opinion_modifier = {
					who = event_target:lover_consort_one
					modifier = opinion_abducted
				}
				reverse_has_opinion_modifier = {
					who = event_target:lover_consort_two
					modifier = opinion_abducted
				}
			}
		}
		
		name = EVTOPTB_TIANXIA_2598 # Maybe we should all be lovers?
		
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:lover_consort_one = {
					is_lover = ROOT
				}
			}
			
			event_target:lover_consort_two = {
				character_event = { id = tianxia.2600 } # Non-lover approached

# Spouse is not the lover of either but tries their luck - lover two
character_event = {
	id = tianxia.2606
	desc = EVTDESC_TIANXIA_2606
	picture = GFX_evt_garden # TIANXIATODO
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion # TIANXIATODO
	
	portrait = event_target:shared_partner
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTA_TIANXIA_2606 # Sure!
		
		add_lover = event_target:shared_partner
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100 # TIANXIATODO: Logic
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTB_TIANXIA_2606 # Nope!
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100 # TIANXIATODO: Logic
		}
	}
	
	after = {
		event_target:shared_partner = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					is_lover = ROOT
					is_lover = event_target:lover_consort_one
				}
				
				character_event = { id = tianxia.2607 } # Both accepted
			}
			
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						is_lover = ROOT
						is_lover = event_target:lover_consort_one
					}
				}
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						is_lover = event_target:lover_consort_one
					}
					
					character_event = { id = tianxia.2608 } # The first lover accepted
				}
				
				else = {
					character_event = { id = tianxia.2609 } # The second lover accepted
				}
			}
			
			else = {
				character_event = { id = tianxia.2610 } # Neither accepted
			}
		}
		
		event_target:lover_consort_one = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					ROOT = {
						is_lover = event_target:shared_partner
					}
				}
				
				character_event = { id = tianxia.2611 } # Lover two accepted
			}
			
			else = {
				character_event = { id = tianxia.2612 } # Lover two refused
			}
		}
		
		event_target:lover_consort_one = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					is_lover = event_target:shared_partner
				}
				
				ROOT = {
					character_event = { id = tianxia.2613 } # Lover one accepted
				}
			}
			
			else = {
				ROOT = {
					character_event = { id = tianxia.2614 } # Lover one refused
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

		event_target:shared_partner = {
			remove_lover = ROOT
			
			character_event = {	id = tianxia.2618 } # Informed of outcome
		}
		
		if = {
			limit = {
				character = event_target:lover_consort_one
			}
			
			event_target:lover_consort_two = {
				character_event = {	id = tianxia.2619 } # Informed of outcome
			}
		}
		
		else = {
			event_target:lover_consort_one = {
				character_event = {	id = tianxia.2619 } # Informed of outcome
			}
		}
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100 # TIANXIATODO: Logic
		}
	}
}

		if = {
			limit = {
				character = event_target:lover_consort_one
			}
			
			event_target:lover_consort_two = {
				save_event_target_as = other_lover
			}
		}
		
		else = {

				save_event_target_as = other_lover
			}
		}
	}

		tooltip = {
			imprison = FROM
		}
	}

# One lover was imprisoned - spouse
character_event = {
	id = tianxia.2630
	desc = EVTDESC_TIANXIA_2630
	picture = GFX_evt_garden # TIANXIATODO
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion # TIANXIATODO
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:lover_consort_one = {
					OR = {
						is_landed = no
						any_liege = {
							character = ROOT
						}
					}
				}
			}
			
			event_target:lover_consort_one = {
				save_event_target_as = imprisoned_lover
			}
			
			event_target:lover_consort_two = {
				save_event_target_as = escaped_lover
			}
		}
		
		else = {
			event_target:lover_consort_one = {
				save_event_target_as = escaped_lover
			}
			
			event_target:lover_consort_two = {
				save_event_target_as = imprisoned_lover
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTA_TIANXIA_2630 # I'll get the other later!
		
		event_target:imprisoned_lover = {
			reverse_imprison = ROOT
			
			character_event = { id = tianxia.2631 } # Inform character
		}
		
		event_target:escaped_lover = {
			character_event = { id = tianxia.2632 } # Inform character
		}
	}
}

# Lovers avoided imprisonment and fled - spouse
character_event = {
	id = tianxia.2633
	desc = EVTDESC_TIANXIA_2633
	picture = GFX_evt_garden # TIANXIATODO
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion # TIANXIATODO
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	immediate = {
		event_target:lover_consort_one = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					is_landed = no
					any_liege = {
						character = ROOT
					}
				}
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						event_target:lover_consort_two = {
							is_landed = yes
						}
					}
					
					move_character = event_target:lover_consort_two
					force_host = event_target:lover_consort_two
				}
				
				else_if = {
					limit = {
						event_target:lover_consort_two = {
							employer = {
								NOT = {
									any_liege = {
										character = ROOT
									}
								}
							}
						}
					}
					
					event_target:lover_consort_two = {
						employer = {
							save_event_target_as = new_host
						}
					}
					
					move_character = event_target:new_host
					force_host = event_target:new_host
				}
				
				else = {
					leave_court_effect = yes
				}
			}
			
			character_event = { id = tianxia.2634 } # Inform lovers
		}
		
		event_target:lover_consort_two = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					is_landed = no
					any_liege = {
						character = ROOT
					}
				}
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						is_landed = no
						any_liege = {
							character = ROOT
						}
					}
					
					if = {
						limit = {
							event_target:lover_consort_one = {
								is_landed = yes
							}
						}
						
						move_character = event_target:lover_consort_one
						force_host = event_target:lover_consort_one
					}
					
					else_if = {
						limit = {
							event_target:lover_consort_one = {
								employer = {
									NOT = {
										any_liege = {
											character = ROOT
										}
									}
								}
							}
						}
						
						event_target:lover_consort_one = {
							employer = {
								save_event_target_as = new_host
							}
						}
						
						move_character = event_target:new_host
						force_host = event_target:new_host
					}
					
					else = {
						leave_court_effect = yes
					}
				}
			}
			
			character_event = { id = tianxia.2634 } # Inform lovers
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTA_TIANXIA_2633 # Curses!
	}
}

# Lovers avoided imprisonment and fled - lovers

	picture = GFX_evt_garden # TIANXIATODO
